Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The harmonic variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 232 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 836 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_232(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 232. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^836(S, F) is trivial. QED.
